# Skills · P · part 24

811 entries, partial-commits to party. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **partial-commits**: Commits only task-relevant changes when multiple agents share a branch, using partial staging and preserving unrelated work. Use before any git commit, when the user asks to commit, when staging changes, when running git add or git commit, or when working changes may include edits from other agents (partial commit, multi-agent, shared branch, git add -p, selective staging). ([chiubaka/circleci-orb](https://github.com/chiubaka/circleci-orb/tree/HEAD/org/agents/skills/partial-commits) · ★1)
- **partial-compile**: Use when running `cargo run -- --compile`, working with .arc/.ars partial-compilation output, modifying src/partial_compiler/ (LLVM codegen, eligibility rules, the typed ABI, or the Rust-crate import[rs] loader), or explaining/demoing how an Arrow (.ar) module gets compiled to native machine code and dispatched natively on import. Includes the canonical physics.ar demo workflow and full codegen implementation reference. ([KoichiYatsuzuka/new_language](https://github.com/KoichiYatsuzuka/new_language/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/partial-compile) · ★2)
- **partial-completion**: Stops all-or-nothing refusals. When only one part of a multi-part request is out of bounds, completes every other part in the same reply, states the one boundary in a single plain sentence, and never discards work the user already paid tokens for. Use whenever a request has several parts, a long task is half done, or a reply is about to be a flat refusal of something that is mostly fine. ([Vumb-VibeCoder/ai-anti-overrefusal-skills](https://github.com/Vumb-VibeCoder/ai-anti-overrefusal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/partial-completion) · ★21)
- **partial-completion-state-reconciliation**: Use when a multi-part task cannot finish every component in one run to create or update a status matrix with dependencies and evidence per subgoal. Use the configured search, fetch, read, browser, and test capabilities only when available and authorized. Success criterion: the final status names completed, blocked, and unverified parts without contradiction. Preserve evidence and a run record, limit rework to three meaningful passes, and obtain approval before externally visible or irreversible writes. ([Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills](https://github.com/Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-agent-systems/agent-architecture-orchestration/partial-completion-state-reconciliation) · ★3)
- **partial-consensus**: Determine consensus/disagreement from 5-agent debate - exposes disagreements as developer decisions ([Tonny-Gu/rdma-examples](https://github.com/Tonny-Gu/rdma-examples/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/partial-consensus))
- **partial-correlation-analysis-of-biomarkers**: Use when testing whether a metabolic biomarker panel adds predictive value beyond conventional risk factors, or when the question is whether risk-factor information is already captured by correlated metabolites (e.g. lipids measured by both clinical chemistry and NMR). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/partial-correlation-analysis-of-biomarkers))
- **partial-correlation-complementarity-assessment**: Use when implementing or validating a greedy feature-selection criterion (e.g., RRCT or an mRMR extension) that must account for feature interactions: candidate features need scoring via conditional relevance given the already-selected subset, not just marginal relevance and pairwise redundancy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/partial-correlation-complementarity-assessment))

- **partial-correlation-confounder-control**: Use when two variant classes (e.g. SNPs vs rearrangements) both correlate with an outcome such as offspring viability and you must estimate each factor's influence alone via partial correlations on unshared-variant distances.
